- **ooad-architect**: C4 (Simon Brown) + UML + RDD/CRC (Wirfs-Brock) + GRASP (Larman) + GoF (Gamma) + Clean Architecture (Robert C. Martin) + ADR-MADR — design with Dependency Rule. - **ooad-requirements**: IEEE 29148 (ex-830) SRS + Use Cases (Cockburn) + RDD/CRC (Wirfs-Brock) + INVEST/Gherkin (Wake/North) + Fagan (Fagan) — Elicitation→Validation with RTM. Use after PRD/Vision exists. ([estebanmatias92/ooad-workflow](https://github.com/estebanmatias92/ooad-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/ooad-requirements) · ★1)
- **ooad-ship**: Continuous Delivery (Humble/Farley) + SRE (Google) — ship via CI/CD pipeline, prod checklist, rollback & changelog. Use after verify has no blockers. ([estebanmatias92/ooad-workflow](https://github.com/estebanmatias92/ooad-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/ooad-ship) · ★1)
- **ooad-verify**: BDD/Gherkin (Dan North) + Test Pyramid (Mike Cohn) 80/15/5 — derive Given/When/Then from SRS/UC/US, RTM-traced. Use after at least one build slice is green. ([estebanmatias92/ooad-workflow](https://github.com/estebanmatias92/ooad-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/ooad-verify) · ★1)
- **oob-detection**: Detect out-of-bounds memory accesses in CPU or GPU code using static interval analysis and runtime assertions/printfs. Use when investigating OOB, buffer overrun, invalid memory access, HIP/ROCm illegal address, CUDA illegal memory access, silent tensor corruption, or suspicious buffer_load/store address arithmetic. ([ROCm/FlyDSL](https://github.com/ROCm/FlyDSL/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/oob-detection) · ★311 · 2 copies)
- **oob-error-convergence-assessment**: Use when fitting a random survival forest on microbiome taxa for survival prediction (MiSurv Module 4) and you must verify OOB error stabilization at a chosen ntree (10,000) before ranking predictors by VIMP. - **oob-error-rate-extraction**: Use when a trained randomForest object built on metabolomics feature data (e.g. the four selected metabolites Adenylosuccinate, Histidine, Hydroxyproline, Linolenic Acid) is available and you need OOB error rates to report or compare predictive performance per group pair. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/oob-error-rate-extraction))
- **oob-exfil-kit**: 授权 RCE 无回显外带：DNS 分片、HTTP curl 包装、解码 qname。 触发：DNS 隧道、OOB、无回显 RCE、dnslog。 默认不绑 53、不洪水。DNSlog ≠ GetShell。 ([th1nk3rrr-dev/TGSEC-Qtzuu](https://github.com/th1nk3rrr-dev/TGSEC-Qtzuu/tree/HEAD/hermes-skills/security/oob-exfil-kit) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **oob-interactions**: Confirm blind vulnerabilities with out-of-band interaction tracking. Use whenever a payload's effect is NOT visible in the HTTP response: blind SSRF, stored/blind XSS, blind XXE, blind RCE/command injection, blind SQLi (DNS exfil), deserialization callbacks, log poisoning with async trigger. Manages unique correlation tokens via engine/oast.py so a callback is evidence-grade (Demonstrated), not background noise. Keywords: OAST, OOB, out-of-band, interactsh, Burp Collaborator, callback, blind, DNS exfiltration, correlation token, listener. Do NOT use for vulnerabilities whose effect is directl… ([mahumadad/quarry](https://github.com/mahumadad/quarry/tree/HEAD/skills/core/oob-interactions) · ★1)
- **oob-perf-analysis**: Generate and analyze T1/T2/R roofline reports for PyTorch OOB workloads comparing Intel XPU and NVIDIA CUDA. Use when working with eager profiling artifacts, per-model reports, fleet summaries, graph consistency, or XPU-vs-CUDA software efficiency analysis. ([intel/torch-xpu-ops](https://github.com/intel/torch-xpu-ops/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/oob-perf-analysis) · ★122)

- **ood**: Object-Oriented Data principles, violation patterns, and compliance test. Use when reviewing code for OOD compliance, designing domain objects, checking for scattered logic in utils/helpers/services, or when user mentions object-oriented data, domain modeling, or translation boundaries. ([Pixley-Growth/cal-plugin](https://github.com/Pixley-Growth/cal-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/ood) · ★3)
- **ood**: Orchestration-Oriented Development lead. Run a feature/epic end-to-end as the team lead: dialogue the spec into a plan, run design -&gt; review -&gt; implement -&gt; inspect with an Agent Team, and gate completion on inspection. Triggers: "/ood", "オーケストレーション", "計画して実装", "plan and build this", "run this as a team", "spec to implementation". The plan and reports under docs/ are the state; do not build a JSON state machine. ([hummer98/ood](https://github.com/hummer98/ood/tree/HEAD/skills/ood))
- **ood-add-feature**: 기존 OOD 설계서가 있는 프로젝트에 새로운 기능의 OOD 초안을 추가한다. 기존 OOD(아키텍처·계층·클래스·인터페이스)와 신규 OOA(유스케이스·도메인 모델·상태 모델)를 동시에 분석하여, 기존 클래스/패키지를 재사용하면서 일관된 설계를 작성한다. 6단계 워크플로우(아키텍처 → Use Case Realization → 책임 할당 → 디자인 패턴 → 설계 클래스 다이어그램 → 영속성·횡단 관심사)를 단계별 확인을 받으며 진행한다. 'OOD 기능 추가', '설계에 추가', '클래스 추가', '기능 설계 확장' 등의 요청에 사용한다. 기존 OOD가 없는 경우에는 ood-new-project 스킬을 사용한다. ([GoGradually/usecase-driven-design-skills](https://github.com/GoGradually/usecase-driven-design-skills/tree/HEAD/usecase-driven-design/skills/ood-add-feature))
- **ood-design-skill**: Quy chuẩn và quy trình phân tích và thiết kế hướng đối tượng (OOAD/OOD), xây dựng mô hình lớp (Domain Model, Design Class Diagram) cho Hệ thống Quản lý Công văn và Văn bản Nội bộ Tích hợp AI. ([maresea/nanana-adomixi](https://github.com/maresea/nanana-adomixi/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ood))
- **ood-detection-3d-medical-eval**: Evaluates the ability of out-of-distribution (OOD) detection methods to identify distribution shifts in 3D medical image segmentation. It measures how well models distinguish in-distribution scans from clinically anomalous or shifted OOD scans, highlighting the limitations of deep learning-based detectors compared to simpler intensity-based baselines. Use when the user wants to benchmark on 3D CT datasets, 3D MRI datasets, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports FPR at 95% TPR (FPR95). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/ood-detection-3d-medical-eval) · ★3)
- **ood-detection-cxr-eval**: Evaluates a model's ability to distinguish in-distribution chest X-rays from out-of-distribution medical images (e.g., knee, hand, or general radiographs) while maintaining classification accuracy on chest diseases. Use when the user wants to benchmark on CXR14, IRMA, MURA, Bone Age, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports AUC. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/ood-detection-cxr-eval) · ★3)
- **ood-detection-eval**: Evaluates a model's ability to distinguish between in-distribution (ID) samples and out-of-distribution (OOD) samples. It measures how well the model's decision boundaries separate known classes from unknown data distributions using various synthetic and natural OOD benchmarks. Use when the user wants to benchmark on SVHN, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, TinyImageNet-crop, TinyImageNet-resize, LSUN-crop, LSUN-resize, iSUN, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports TNR@TPR95. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/ood-detection-eval) · ★3)
- **ood-detection-histopathology-eval**: Evaluates the ability of uncertainty estimation methods to distinguish in-distribution clinical histopathology samples from out-of-distribution samples and to provide well-calibrated confidence scores for selective prediction. It probes how different methods maintain predictive accuracy and calibration under varying degrees of data shift. Use when the user wants to benchmark on CIFAR-10, Hospital 4, Hospital 5, Cancer type, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/ood-detection-histopathology-eval) · ★3)
- **ood-guard**: Classify whether a query condition falls inside the region a model was actually trained on, returning pass / controlled / reject per point rather than a vague extrapolation warning. Use before trusting any prediction or surrogate output on conditions outside the training range, when estimating how far a proposed experiment is from existing data, or when deciding whether a model can answer at all or should refuse. Backed by a live distance-aware guard endpoint over 63 literature-referenced scenarios. Not for wet-lab safety review and not a replacement for a physical risk assessment. ([lm203688/swarmlabs-skills](https://github.com/lm203688/swarmlabs-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ood-guard))
- **ood-mol-opt-eval**: Out-of-domain molecular property prediction and Bayesian optimization for molecular design. Tests transferability of learned representations to novel tasks. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Out-of-domain molecular design tasks, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Top performing molecule property. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/ood-mol-opt-eval) · ★3)
- **ood-new-project**: OOA(유스케이스 주도 분석)가 완료된 프로젝트에 객체지향 설계(OOD) 초안을 처음 만든다. 6단계 워크플로우(아키텍처·계층 분해 → Use Case Realization → GRASP 책임 할당 → GoF 디자인 패턴 → 설계 클래스 다이어그램(DCD) → 영속성·횡단 관심사)를 단계별 확인을 받으며 진행한다. 각 단계 산출물은 개별 마크다운 파일에 기록하고, 완료 후 하나의 설계 초안 파일로 통합한다. 'OOD 시작', '설계 초안', '클래스 설계', '아키텍처 설계', 'design 만들기' 등의 요청에 사용한다. OOA 산출물(docs/usecase/*-usecase-design.md 또는 docs/usecase/drafts/[feature]/[feature]-draft.md)이 반드시 존재해야 한다. 기존 OOD 문서에 신규 기능을 추가하는 경우에는 ood-add-feature 스킬을 사용한다. ([GoGradually/usecase-driven-design-skills](https://github.com/GoGradually/usecase-driven-design-skills/tree/HEAD/usecase-driven-design/skills/ood-new-project))
- **ood-pointcloud-seg-eval**: Evaluates a model's ability to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs in 3D point cloud semantic segmentation. It probes domain shift robustness (indoor vs outdoor scenes) and sensor failure simulation (missing color channels) by measuring uncertainty-based OOD scores against in-distribution data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Semantic3D, S3DIS, Semantic3D (no color), or asks about evaluating this task. Reports AUROC. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/ood-pointcloud-seg-eval) · ★3)
- **ood-review**: OOD 설계 초안 또는 메인 설계서를 6관점에서 리뷰한다. 완전성, OOA 정합성(추적성), SOLID 준수, GRASP 적용 적절성, 결합도·응집도·의존성 방향, 디자인 패턴 적용 타당성을 체계적으로 검토하고 개선점을 제안한다. 리뷰 결과는 마크다운 파일에 기록한다. 'OOD 리뷰', '설계 검토', '클래스 다이어그램 점검', '아키텍처 리뷰' 등의 요청에 사용한다. 리뷰 결과에 따라 수정을 바로 반영하거나 리뷰 리포트만 제공할 수 있다. - **ope-rs-info-retrieval**: 当用户想要检索离线策略评估（OPE）/离线强化学习/决策智能相关的学术论文、代码仓库或两者兼有时自动触发。常见场景：用户说"搜 IPS/DR/WDR 估计器相关论文"、"找 OPE benchmark 代码"、"查离线 RL 最新进展"、"帮我找 OPE 数据集"。本 skill 并行检索多个平台、去重排序后输出结构化清单，不做深度精读（那是 ope-rs-literature-filter 的工作）。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/ope/ope-rs-info-retrieval) · ★16)
- **ope-rs-literature-filter**: 当用户想要对 OPE/离线 RL 论文列表进行筛选、精读、制作文献矩阵或文献综述表时自动触发。常见场景：用户说"帮我筛选这些 OPE 论文"、"对这些 IPS 估计器论文做精读摘要"、"制作一个 OPE 方法对比矩阵"。本 skill 依赖 ope-rs-info-retrieval 的检索结果，不负责新检索。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/ope/ope-rs-literature-filter) · ★16)
- **ope-rs-paper-revision**: 当用户想要修改润色 OPE 论文、选定期刊投稿、或回复审稿意见时自动触发。常见场景：用户说"修改论文"、"润色"、"选期刊"、"投稿"、"回复审稿人"。本 skill 完成内部审阅、投稿准备、审稿意见回复，不负责论文初稿撰写。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/ope/ope-rs-paper-revision) · ★16)
- **ope-rs-paper-writing**: 当用户想要撰写或润色 OPE 学术论文时自动触发。常见场景：用户说"写论文"、"润色论文"、"撰写引言"、"写方法部分"、"用 LaTeX 写论文"。本 skill 撰写引言/方法/结果/讨论各章节，支持 LaTeX 与 Markdown 两种格式，不负责内部审阅与投稿。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/ope/ope-rs-paper-writing) · ★16)
- **ope-rs-question-formulation**: 当用户想要凝练 OPE 研究问题、或围绕研究想法展开头脑风暴时自动触发。常见场景：用户说"帮我从这些研究空白里提炼出具体的研究问题"、"我想做一个 OPE 估计器改进研究，帮我细化"、"评估下这个想法的可行性"、"头脑风暴 OPE 还能做什么"。本 skill 依赖 ope-rs-gap-analysis 的产出，不负责文献检索与筛选。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/ope/ope-rs-question-formulation) · ★16)
